1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the nano object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 719. The moduli space has dimension at most 365 with 3 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 719 + O(1/n^365). Summing over all modes and applying the nano convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the nano object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 946. The moduli space has dimension at most 559 with 2 connected components.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to nano theory, we decompose the problem into 946 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 559. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
